> AGENT

Here's the picture:

**PR #449 changes:** `ipns.service.ts`, `fuse/mod.rs`, desktop E2E workflow — none of which touch the web E2E tests or the web app.

**Cascade analysis:**

| Failed setup test | Tests in suite | Skipped due to cascade |
|---|---|---|
| invite-link-workflow (account creation) | 29 | ~28 |
| writable-shares (account creation) | 57 | ~56 |
| mfa-flows (wallet login) | 16 | ~15 |
| media-preview (upload fixtures) | 14 | ~13 |
| recovery (1 standalone) | 13 | ~12 |

So **~91 skipped = cascading from 5 setup failures**. The actual unique failures are:

1. **3 wallet login timeouts** (invite-link, writable-shares, mfa) — same root cause, likely flaky local Web3Auth/wallet mock
2. **media-preview upload** — probably also env-related
3. **recovery** — empty progress log, mock IPNS routing issue (the `POST /api/v0/name/resolve` 404 in the logs)
4. **conflict-detection** — possibly related to the PR's IPNS sequence changes
5. **full-workflow breadcrumb** — unclear

Only **conflict-detection** could plausibly be related to the PR changes (IPNS sequence handling). The rest look like local environment flakiness — especially the 3 wallet login timeouts which are a known pattern.

Want me to re-run just the conflict-detection test to see if it's a real regression from this branch?

```
Perfect! Now I have a clear picture. Let me create a comprehensive summary of the findings:

## Summary: PR #448 Changes Breaking Web E2E Tests

Based on my thorough analysis of PR #448 ("feat: IPNS signature storage and verification"), here are the key changes and potential breaking issues:

### New Required/Expected Fields in IPNS Publish Flow

#### 1. **New Field: `publicKey` (Optional but Recommended)**
   - **Added to DTOs**: `PublishIpnsDto` and `PublishIpnsEntryDto` in `/apps/api/src/ipns/dto/publish.dto.ts`
   - **Location**: Lines 45-54 and 157-166 respectively
   - Type: `string` (base64-encoded raw 32-byte Ed25519 public key)
   - Validation: `@IsBase64()`, `@MaxLength(100)`
   - **Status**: OPTIONAL but there's new server-side validation when provided

#### 2. **Server-Side Validation Changes**
   In `/apps/api/src/ipns/ipns.service.ts` (lines 67-84), when `publicKey` is provided:
   - Must be exactly 32 bytes when decoded
   - Must cryptographically derive to the claimed `ipnsName` using `deriveIpnsName()`
   - If validation fails: **`BadRequestException`** with message "publicKey does not correspond to the given ipnsName"
   - Existing records must match: publicKey cannot change (lines 240-242)

#### 3. **Database Schema Changes**
   Two new nullable columns added to `folder_ipns` table:
   - `signed_record` (bytea) - stores the canonical signed IPNS record bytes
   - `public_key` (bytea) - stores the raw Ed25519 public key
   - **Migrations**: 
     - `1743200000000-AddSignedRecordToFolderIpns.ts`
     - `1743300000000-AddPublicKeyToFolderIpns.ts`

#### 4. **Web Client Implementation** 
   In `/apps/web/src/services/ipns.service.ts`:
   - `createAndPublishIpnsRecord()` now derives and sends publicKey (lines 57-69)
   - Uses `deriveEd25519PublicKey()` from `@cipherbox/crypto`
   - Loop-based base64 encoding (not spread operator) to avoid call-stack overflow
   - **Critical PR review fix**: "Forward publicKey in web app batch publish (was missing vs single publish)"
     - The batch publish was initially missing publicKey forwarding, which was corrected

#### 5. **MaxLength Validation Fix**
   - `ipnsName` field: Changed from `@MaxLength(70)` to `@MaxLength(76)` 
   - Affects both `PublishIpnsDto` and `PublishIpnsEntryDto`
   - This was documented in the security review: "Fix MaxLength mismatch on ipnsName in publish DTOs (70 -> 76)"

### Potential Breaking Points for E2E Tests

1. **If tests manually construct publish requests without publicKey**:
   - Requests still work (publicKey is optional)
   - BUT: If tests then verify stored records, they won't have `publicKey` or `signedRecord` fields
   - This could break tests that expect these signature fields in resolve responses

2. **If tests use hardcoded IPNS names**:
   - The server now validates that provided publicKey derives to the claimed ipnsName
   - Mismatched pairs will be rejected with 400 BadRequest

3. **If tests don't regenerate API client**:
   - The generated TypeScript models in `packages/api-client/src/models/` were updated
   - Old API client code won't know about the new `publicKey` field

4. **Sequence number conflict detection**:
   - New `expectedSequenceNumber` parameter (added earlier) is now more critical
   - Tests that publish multiple times without conflict detection might fail if device-out-of-sync detection is enabled

### Key Findings from Code Review

1. **Security-Critical**: Server validates publicKey-to-ipnsName binding (lines 79-83 of ipns.service.ts)
2. **Backward Compatible**: publicKey is optional, so existing clients still work
3. **DB Enhancement**: `signedRecord` field enables clients to verify signatures against cached data
4. **Signature Verification**: `verifyIpnsSignature()` in web client (lines 139-151) now verifies Ed25519 signatures
5. **Resolve Response Enrichment**: When network and DB have same sequence, signature fields are merged from cache (lines 441-448 of ipns.service.ts)

### Recommended E2E Test Fixes

1. Ensure web client calls `deriveEd25519PublicKey()` and includes `publicKey` in all publish requests
2. Verify API client is regenerated from latest OpenAPI spec
3. If testing signature verification, ensure resolve responses include all three fields: `signatureV2`, `data`, `pubKey`
4. Check for any mock/test data that hardcodes IPNS names without matching public keys
5. Update test fixtures to use correct derived IPNS names (as was done in PR review: "fix(tests): use correct IPNS name derived from test public key")
```

```
## Summary of Web E2E Test Failures Analysis

Based on my investigation, I've identified the root cause and related issues with PR #448 ("feat: IPNS signature storage and verification"). Here's what I found:

### Key Findings:

**1. PR #448 Changes to IPNS Publishing**

PR #448 added public key verification to IPNS records by:
- Adding `publicKey` parameter to IPNS publish endpoints (`createAndPublishIpnsRecord`, `batchPublishIpnsRecords`)
- Server-side validation that the public key cryptographically derives to the claimed IPNS name
- Storing `signedRecord`, `publicKey`, and `sequenceNumber` in the database
- Adding signature verification on resolution

**2. The Sequence Number Bug (Fixed in c6d5cbaf3)**

The follow-up fix `c6d5cbaf3` ("fix(api,desktop): fix sequence number mismatch in cached IPNS resolves") revealed a critical issue:

When a folder IPNS record is published with `sequenceNumber: 0n` (as happens during vault initialization), the API's `upsertFolderIpns` increments it to 1 for storage. However, the record bytes themselves still contain sequence 0 (the client's pre-increment value).

The API was using `parseIpnsRecordBytes()` which extracted sequence 0 from the record, causing `resolveIpnsRecord()` to return sequence 0 instead of 1. This led to 409 conflicts on the next write operation.

**3. Why Tests Are Timing Out During Account Creation**

The web E2E tests are failing specifically during account creation (multi-account-wallet tests timeout at line 152) because:

1. **For new users**, `initializeOrLoadVault()` in `useAuth.ts` (lines 166-223):
   - Calls `createAndPublishIpnsRecord` TWICE:
     - Line 178: Publishes vault key blob IPNS record  
     - Line 195: Publishes root folder metadata IPNS record
   - Both publish with `sequenceNumber: 0n` and `expectedSequenceNumber: undefined`
   - Then calls `vaultApi.initVault()` at line 207 to register with backend

2. **During vault loading**, the resolve path (lines 140-152):
   - Line 142: Calls `resolveIpnsRecord(vaultKeyKeypair.ipnsName)` 
   - This hits the API's `parseCachedRecord()` which was returning the wrong sequence number

3. **The timeout cascade**:
   - Each test creates 2-3 accounts sequentially
   - Each account creation publishes 2 IPNS records
   - If sequence number resolution is incorrect, subsequent operations might timeout or conflict
   - This directly affects the account creation timeout in tests like:
     - `invite-link-workflow.spec.ts:152` - Create test accounts (Alice, Dave, Eve)
     - `writable-shares.spec.ts:115` - Create test accounts (Alice, Bob)

### Test Files and Their Failure Points:

1. **conflict-detection.spec.ts:152** - Uploads file with stale sequence
   - Depends on vault initialization with correct sequence tracking

2. **full-workflow.spec.ts:634** - Breadcrumb navigation after reload
   - Depends on vault loading reading correct IPNS sequence numbers

3. **invite-link-workflow.spec.ts:152** - Create test accounts (timeout)
   - Creates 3 new accounts = 6 IPNS publishes + resolves
   - Likely hitting IPNS sequence mismatch on vault load

4. **media-preview.spec.ts:54** - Upload media fixtures
   - New account creation + uploads

5. **mfa-flows.spec.ts:58** - Wallet login and MFA enrollment
   - New account creation with MFA

6. **recovery.spec.ts:52** - IPFS-direct v2 blob path recovery
   - Uses SDK's `createTestAccount` which publishes vault key blob

7. **writable-shares.spec.ts:115** - Create test accounts (timeout)
   - Creates 2 new accounts = 4 IPNS publishes + resolves

### Test Setup Files Location:

- **Wallet login**: `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/tests/web-e2e/utils/wallet-login-helpers.ts`
  - `loginViaWallet()` (line 111): Waits for `/files` redirect, which depends on vault initialization completing
  - `setupMockWallet()` (line 65): Installs mock wallet before navigation

- **Multi-account creation**: `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/tests/web-e2e/utils/multi-account-wallet.ts`
  - `createWalletTestAccount()` (line 63): Creates account, calls `loginViaWallet()`, waits up to 90 seconds
  - `createWalletTestAccounts()` (line 105): Creates multiple accounts sequentially

- **App initialization**: `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useAuth.ts`
  - `initializeOrLoadVault()` (line 107): Vault init/load flow, calls `createAndPublishIpnsRecord()` twice for new users, then `vaultApi.initVault()`

- **IPNS operations**: `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/ipns.service.ts`
  - `createAndPublishIpnsRecord()` (line 37): Publishes record via API, includes `publicKey` parameter
  - `resolveIpnsRecord()` (line 163): Resolves IPNS, verifies signatures, derives IPNS name from public key

### Mock IPNS Service:

Located at `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/tools/mock-ipns-routing/src/index.ts`, it:
- Stores IPNS records in-memory
- Responds to PUT/GET for `/routing/v1/ipns/{name}`
- Provides `/reset` endpoint for test cleanup
- Started automatically by Playwright config (line 69)

### The Fix Already Applied:

Commit `c6d5cbaf3` fixed the immediate sequence number issue by:
- Using `cached.sequenceNumber` (from DB) instead of parsing from `signedRecord` bytes
- Seeding the Desktop PublishCoordinator cache during FUSE mount pre-population

However, **the web E2E tests may still be experiencing timeout issues** if:
1. The API fix wasn't deployed to the test environment
2. There's a race condition between vault initialization publishes and resolves
3. The mock IPNS service isn't properly handling concurrent operations
```

```
1	/**
2	 * IPNS Service - Record creation, publishing, and resolution
3	 *
4	 * Creates IPNS records locally using @cipherbox/core and publishes
5	 * via the backend API relay to the delegated routing service.
6	 */
7	
8	import { createIpnsRecord, marshalIpnsRecord, IPNS_SIGNATURE_PREFIX } from '@cipherbox/core';
9	import {
10	  verifyEd25519,
11	  concatBytes,
12	  deriveIpnsName,
13	  deriveEd25519PublicKey,
14	} from '@cipherbox/crypto';
15	import {
16	  ipnsControllerPublishRecord,
17	  ipnsControllerPublishBatch,
18	  ipnsControllerResolveRecord,
19	} from '@cipherbox/api-client';
20	import type { PublishIpnsEntryDtoRecordType } from '@cipherbox/api-client';
21	import { logger } from '../lib/logger';
22	
23	/**
24	 * Create an IPNS record locally and publish via backend.
25	 *
26	 * The record is signed locally using the Ed25519 private key, then
27	 * the backend relays it to the IPFS network via delegated routing.
28	 *
29	 * @param params.ipnsPrivateKey - 32-byte Ed25519 private key seed
30	 * @param params.ipnsName - IPNS name (k51.../bafzaa... format)
31	 * @param params.metadataCid - CID of the encrypted metadata blob
32	 * @param params.sequenceNumber - Sequence number for this publish
33	 * @param params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey - Hex ECIES-wrapped key for TEE (first publish only)
34	 * @param params.keyEpoch - TEE key epoch (required with encryptedIpnsPrivateKey)
35	 * @param params.expectedSequenceNumber - Pre-increment sequence number for conflict detection (folder records only)
36	 */
37	export async function createAndPublishIpnsRecord(params: {
38	  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
39	  ipnsName: string;
40	  metadataCid: string;
41	  sequenceNumber: bigint;
42	  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
43	  keyEpoch?: number;
44	  expectedSequenceNumber?: string;
45	}): Promise<{ success: boolean; sequenceNumber: bigint }> {
46	  // 1. Create IPNS record pointing to /ipfs/{metadataCid}
47	  // 24 hour lifetime (will be republished by TEE every 3 hours)
48	  const record = await createIpnsRecord(
49	    params.ipnsPrivateKey,
50	    `/ipfs/${params.metadataCid}`,
51	    params.sequenceNumber,
52	    24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 // 24 hours in ms
53	  );
54	
55	  // 2. Marshal to bytes for transport
56	  const recordBytes = marshalIpnsRecord(record);
57	  const publicKeyBytes = deriveEd25519PublicKey(params.ipnsPrivateKey);
58	
59	  // 3. Base64 encode for API transmission (loop-based to avoid call stack overflow on large records)
60	  let recordBinary = '';
61	  for (let i = 0; i < recordBytes.length; i++) {
62	    recordBinary += String.fromCharCode(recordBytes[i]);
63	  }
64	  const recordBase64 = btoa(recordBinary);
65	  let pkBinary = '';
66	  for (let i = 0; i < publicKeyBytes.length; i++) {
67	    pkBinary += String.fromCharCode(publicKeyBytes[i]);
68	  }
69	  const publicKey = btoa(pkBinary);
70	
71	  // 4. Call backend API to relay to IPFS network
72	  const response = await ipnsControllerPublishRecord({
73	    ipnsName: params.ipnsName,
74	    record: recordBase64,
75	    publicKey,
76	    metadataCid: params.metadataCid,
77	    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
78	    keyEpoch: params.keyEpoch,
79	    expectedSequenceNumber: params.expectedSequenceNumber,
80	  });
81	
82	  return {
83	    success: response.success,
84	    sequenceNumber: BigInt(response.sequenceNumber),
85	  };
86	}
87	
88	/**
89	 * Batch publish multiple IPNS records in a single API call.
90	 *
91	 * Sends all records (folder and/or file) to the batch endpoint,
92	 * which processes them with concurrency-limited parallelism.
93	 * Partial success is allowed: individual failures do not fail the batch.
94	 *
95	 * @param records - Array of IPNS record payloads to publish
96	 * @returns Success and failure counts
97	 */
98	export async function batchPublishIpnsRecords(
99	  records: Array<{
100	    ipnsName: string;
101	    recordBase64: string;
102	    publicKey?: string;
103	    metadataCid: string;
104	    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
105	    keyEpoch?: number;
106	    recordType?: 'folder' | 'file';
107	    /** Pre-increment sequence number for conflict detection (folder records only) */
108	    expectedSequenceNumber?: string;
109	  }>
110	): Promise<{ totalSucceeded: number; totalFailed: number }> {
111	  const response = await ipnsControllerPublishBatch({
112	    records: records.map((r) => ({
113	      ipnsName: r.ipnsName,
114	      record: r.recordBase64,
115	      publicKey: r.publicKey,
116	      metadataCid: r.metadataCid,
117	      encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: r.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
118	      keyEpoch: r.keyEpoch,
119	      recordType: r.recordType as PublishIpnsEntryDtoRecordType | undefined,
120	      expectedSequenceNumber: r.expectedSequenceNumber,
121	    })),
122	  });
123	
124	  return {
125	    totalSucceeded: response.totalSucceeded,
126	    totalFailed: response.totalFailed,
127	  };
128	}
129	
130	/**
131	 * Verify the Ed25519 signature on an IPNS record.
132	 * Per IPFS spec, the signature is over "ipns-signature:" + cborData.
133	 *
134	 * @param signatureV2 - base64 Ed25519 signature (64 bytes)
135	 * @param data - base64 CBOR data that was signed
136	 * @param pubKey - base64 raw Ed25519 public key (32 bytes)
137	 * @returns true if valid
138	 */
139	export async function verifyIpnsSignature(
140	  signatureV2: string,
141	  data: string,
142	  pubKey: string
143	): Promise<boolean> {
144	  const sigBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(signatureV2), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
145	  const dataBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(data), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
146	  const pubKeyBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(pubKey), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
147	
148	  // Per IPFS IPNS spec, signature is over "ipns-signature:" + cborData
149	  const signedData = concatBytes(IPNS_SIGNATURE_PREFIX, dataBytes);
150	  return verifyEd25519(sigBytes, signedData, pubKeyBytes);
151	}
152	
153	/**
154	 * Resolve an IPNS name to its current CID and sequence number.
155	 *
156	 * Calls backend API which relays to the delegated routing service for resolution.
157	 * When the response includes IPNS signature data (from delegated routing),
158	 * verifies the Ed25519 signature before trusting the CID.
159	 *
160	 * @param ipnsName - IPNS name to resolve (k51.../bafzaa... format)
161	 * @returns Current CID, sequence number, and signature verification status, or null if not found
162	 */
163	export async function resolveIpnsRecord(
164	  ipnsName: string
165	): Promise<{ cid: string; sequenceNumber: bigint; signatureVerified: boolean } | null> {
166	  try {
167	    const response = await ipnsControllerResolveRecord({ ipnsName });
168	
169	    if (!response.success) {
170	      return null;
171	    }
172	
173	    // Verify IPNS signature if all signature fields are present
174	    let signatureVerified = false;
175	    if (response.signatureV2 && response.data && response.pubKey) {
176	      const valid = await verifyIpnsSignature(response.signatureV2, response.data, response.pubKey);
177	      if (!valid) {
178	        throw new Error('IPNS signature verification failed - record may be tampered');
179	      }
180	
181	      // Verify the returned public key derives to the requested IPNS name
182	      const pubKeyBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(response.pubKey), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
183	      const derivedName = await deriveIpnsName(pubKeyBytes);
184	      if (derivedName !== ipnsName) {
185	        throw new Error(
186	          'IPNS public key does not match requested name - possible key substitution'
187	        );
188	      }
189	
190	      signatureVerified = true;
191	    } else {
192	      logger.warn('[IPNS] IPNS resolve returned without signature data, skipping verification');
193	    }
194	
195	    return {
196	      cid: response.cid,
197	      sequenceNumber: BigInt(response.sequenceNumber),
198	      signatureVerified,
199	    };
200	  } catch (error) {
201	    // 404 means IPNS name not found - return null
202	    // Other errors should propagate (including signature verification failures)
203	    if (error instanceof Error && (error as Error & { status?: number }).status === 404) {
204	      return null;
205	    }
206	    throw error;
207	  }
208	}
209	
```

```
1	/**
2	 * Recovery Tool E2E Test
3	 *
4	 * Seeds a vault with a file via SDK, then uses recovery.html to verify
5	 * the IPFS-direct v2 blob recovery path works end-to-end.
6	 *
7	 * Requires: API + IPFS running locally (same as other web-e2e tests).
8	 */
9	import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
10	import {
11	  createTestAccount,
12	  deleteTestAccount,
13	  type TestAccount,
14	} from '../../sdk-e2e/src/fixtures/test-harness';
15	import { bytesToHex } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
16	
17	const API_URL = process.env.SDK_E2E_API_URL?.trim() || 'http://localhost:3000';
18	const WEB_URL = process.env.WEB_URL?.trim() || 'http://localhost:5173';
19	const IPFS_GATEWAY = process.env.RECOVERY_IPFS_GATEWAY?.trim() || 'http://localhost:8080';
20	// IPNS resolution via delegated routing service (Kubo can't resolve IPNS names published
21	// through it, and the CipherBox API requires auth). Derives from DELEGATED_ROUTING_URL
22	// used by the API, so it stays in sync if the service runs on a different port.
23	const IPNS_GATEWAY =
24	  process.env.RECOVERY_IPNS_GATEWAY?.trim() ||
25	  process.env.DELEGATED_ROUTING_URL?.trim() ||
26	  'http://localhost:3001';
27	
28	// Recovery involves multiple IPNS resolutions + IPFS fetches + decryption.
29	// Keep test timeout and assertion timeout in sync so the assertion doesn't
30	// expire before the test itself does.
31	const RECOVERY_TIMEOUT_MS = 90_000;
32	
33	test.describe('Vault Recovery Tool', () => {
34	  let account: TestAccount;
35	  const testFileName = `recovery-test-${Date.now()}.txt`;
36	  const testFileContent = new TextEncoder().encode('CipherBox recovery test file content');
37	
38	  test.beforeAll(async () => {
39	    // Seed: create test account and upload a file
40	    account = await createTestAccount({
41	      apiUrl: API_URL,
42	      label: `recovery-e2e-${Date.now()}`,
43	      emailPrefix: 'recovery',
44	    });
45	
46	    // Upload a test file to the root folder
47	    await account.client.uploadFile(
48	      account.rootIpnsName,
49	      testFileContent,
50	      testFileName,
51	      'text/plain'
52	    );
53	
54	    // Brief wait for IPNS propagation to DB cache
55	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2000));
56	  });
57	
58	  test.afterAll(async () => {
59	    if (account) {
60	      account.client.destroy();
61	      account.privateKey.fill(0);
62	      account.rootFolderKey.fill(0);
63	      account.rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey.fill(0);
64	      await deleteTestAccount(account, API_URL);
65	    }
66	  });
67	
68	  test('recovers vault files via IPFS-direct v2 blob path', async ({ page }) => {
69	    test.setTimeout(RECOVERY_TIMEOUT_MS);
70	    // Navigate to recovery tool
71	    await page.goto(`${WEB_URL}/recovery.html`);
72	
73	    // Enter private key
74	    const privateKeyHex = bytesToHex(account.privateKey);
75	    await page.locator('[data-testid="recovery-key-input"]').fill(privateKeyHex);
76	
77	    // Set gateway URLs
78	    await page.locator('[data-testid="recovery-ipfs-gateway"]').clear();
79	    await page.locator('[data-testid="recovery-ipfs-gateway"]').fill(IPFS_GATEWAY);
80	
81	    await page.locator('[data-testid="recovery-ipns-gateway"]').clear();
82	    await page.locator('[data-testid="recovery-ipns-gateway"]').fill(IPNS_GATEWAY);
83	
84	    // Click start recovery
85	    await page.locator('[data-testid="recovery-start-btn"]').click();
86	
87	    // Wait for recovery to complete (progress log should show file name)
88	    // Recovery involves: IPNS resolution + IPFS fetch + v2 blob decrypt + folder traversal
89	    // This can take up to 60 seconds depending on IPNS propagation
90	    const progressLog = page.locator('[data-testid="recovery-progress-log"]');
91	
92	    // Wait for the progress log to contain the file name or a success indicator
93	    // The recovery tool logs each discovered file
94	    await expect(progressLog).toContainText(testFileName, {
95	      timeout: RECOVERY_TIMEOUT_MS - 10_000,
96	    });
97	
98	    // Verify the download button becomes visible (recovery succeeded)
99	    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid="recovery-download-btn"]')).toBeVisible({
100	      timeout: 10_000,
101	    });
102	  });
103	});
```

```
1	import { type BrowserContext, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
2	import { installMockWallet } from '@johanneskares/wallet-mock';
3	import { generatePrivateKey, privateKeyToAccount, type PrivateKeyAccount } from 'viem/accounts';
4	import { mainnet } from 'viem/chains';
5	import { custom } from 'viem';
6	
7	/**
8	 * Result of a wallet login attempt.
9	 * - `success`: Login completed, page redirected to /files.
10	 * - `requiredShare`: MFA is enabled and this device needs an additional share
11	 *   (DeviceWaitingScreen is shown).
12	 */
13	export type WalletLoginResult = { outcome: 'success' } | { outcome: 'requiredShare' };
14	
15	/**
16	 * Local-only transport for the mock wallet.
17	 *
18	 * The mock wallet from @johanneskares/wallet-mock doesn't explicitly handle
19	 * `eth_chainId` — unhandled methods fall through to the configured viem
20	 * transport. Using `http()` (the default mainnet RPC) causes `eth_chainId`
21	 * to make a real HTTP request, which hangs in CI where the RPC may be
22	 * unreachable. This custom transport returns mock data for all methods
23	 * the mock wallet doesn't handle itself, avoiding any network calls.
24	 */
25	const localTransport = custom({
26	  async request({ method }) {
27	    switch (method) {
28	      case 'eth_chainId':
29	        return '0x1'; // mainnet
30	      case 'net_version':
31	        return '1';
32	      case 'eth_blockNumber':
33	        return '0x0';
34	      case 'eth_getBalance':
35	        return '0x0';
36	      default:
37	        throw new Error(`Mock transport: unhandled method ${method}`);
38	    }
39	  },
40	});
41	
42	/**
43	 * Generate a random viem account for MFA tests.
44	 *
45	 * Each test run uses a unique private key → unique wallet address →
46	 * unique userId in the backend → fresh DKG identity on Sapphire Devnet.
47	 * This ensures no pre-existing MFA state from previous runs.
48	 *
49	 * The account should be shared across all tests in a serial suite so
50	 * that the same Core Kit identity is used throughout.
51	 */
52	export function createTestAccount(): PrivateKeyAccount {
53	  return privateKeyToAccount(generatePrivateKey());
54	}
55	
56	/**
57	 * Set up a mock wallet on a page (must be called BEFORE navigation).
58	 *
59	 * Creates an EIP-6963 mock provider that auto-announces itself.
60	 * wagmi's injected() connector auto-discovers it.
61	 *
62	 * @param page - Playwright page
63	 * @param account - viem account to use for signing (all contexts must share the same account)
64	 */
65	export async function setupMockWallet(page: Page, account: PrivateKeyAccount): Promise<void> {
66	  await installMockWallet({
67	    page,
68	    account,
69	    defaultChain: mainnet,
70	    transports: { [mainnet.id]: localTransport },
71	  });
72	}
73	
74	/**
75	 * Create a new browser context with a mock wallet installed.
76	 *
77	 * Returns the context and its first page, both with the mock wallet
78	 * ready. The context must be closed by the caller.
79	 *
80	 * @param browser - Playwright browser instance
81	 * @param account - viem account to use (same account across all contexts)
82	 */
83	export async function createWalletContext(
84	  browser: { newContext: () => Promise<BrowserContext> },
85	  account: PrivateKeyAccount
86	): Promise<{ context: BrowserContext; page: Page }> {
87	  const context = await browser.newContext();
88	  const page = await context.newPage();
89	  await setupMockWallet(page, account);
90	  return { context, page };
91	}
92	
93	/**
94	 * Perform a full wallet login via the UI.
95	 *
96	 * Drives the real wallet -> SIWE -> Core Kit flow:
97	 * 1. Navigate to login page
98	 * 2. Wait for Core Kit init (wallet button becomes enabled)
99	 * 3. Click [WALLET] button -> connector list appears
100	 * 4. Select "Mock Wallet" connector
101	 * 5. Wait for either:
102	 *    - /files redirect (success, no MFA or device already known)
103	 *    - DeviceWaitingScreen (MFA enabled, new device needs share)
104	 *
105	 * The mock wallet auto-approves connect + signMessage, so the SIWE
106	 * flow proceeds without user interaction.
107	 *
108	 * @param page - Page with mock wallet already installed via setupMockWallet()
109	 * @param options.timeout - Max time to wait for login outcome (default: 60s)
110	 */
111	export async function loginViaWallet(
112	  page: Page,
113	  options: { timeout?: number } = {}
114	): Promise<WalletLoginResult> {
115	  const timeout = options.timeout ?? 60_000;
116	
117	  // 1. Navigate to login page
118	  await page.goto('/');
119	
120	  // 2. Wait for Core Kit to initialize (wallet button becomes enabled)
121	  const walletButton = page.locator('[data-testid="wallet-login-button"]');
122	  await walletButton.waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 20_000 });
123	  // Poll until the button is enabled (Core Kit init can take several seconds)
124	  await page.waitForFunction(
125	    () => {
126	      const btn = document.querySelector(
127	        '[data-testid="wallet-login-button"]'
128	      ) as HTMLButtonElement;
129	      return btn && !btn.disabled;
130	    },
131	    { timeout: 20_000 }
132	  );
133	
134	  // 3. Click wallet button to show connector list
135	  await walletButton.click();
136	
137	  // 4. Select Mock Wallet from the connector list
138	  const connectorList = page.locator('.wallet-connector-list');
139	  await connectorList.waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 5_000 });
140	  const mockWalletOption = page.locator('.wallet-connector-option', { hasText: 'Mock Wallet' });
141	  await mockWalletOption.click();
142	
143	  // 5. Wait for outcome: either /files redirect or DeviceWaitingScreen
144	  const result = await Promise.race([
145	    page.waitForURL('**/files', { timeout }).then(() => ({ outcome: 'success' as const })),
146	    page
147	      .locator('[data-testid="device-waiting"]')
148	      .waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout })
149	      .then(() => ({ outcome: 'requiredShare' as const })),
150	  ]);
151	
152	  // 6. If login succeeded, wait for vault + SDK initialization to complete.
153	  // The file list or empty state appearing proves that vault keys loaded,
154	  // IPNS metadata resolved, and the SDK client is ready for operations.
155	  if (result.outcome === 'success') {
156	    await Promise.race([
157	      page.locator('.file-list[role="grid"]').waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 30_000 }),
158	      page.locator('[data-testid="empty-state"]').waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 30_000 }),
159	    ]);
160	  }
161	
162	  return result;
163	}
164	
```

```
8	 * Each account has an isolated browser context, a random EVM identity,
9	 * and its public key extracted from the Settings page UI after login.
10	 */
11	export interface WalletTestAccount {
12	  name: string;
13	  context: BrowserContext;
14	  page: Page;
15	  account: PrivateKeyAccount;
16	  /** 0x04-prefixed secp256k1 public key (for sharing — extracted from Settings page) */
17	  publicKey: string;
18	}
19	
20	/**
21	 * Extract the user's public key from the Settings page UI.
22	 *
23	 * Navigates to /settings, reads the key from the visible element,
24	 * then navigates back to /files. This avoids reading Zustand internals.
25	 */
26	async function extractPublicKeyFromUI(page: Page): Promise<string> {
27	  // Navigate to settings
28	  await page.evaluate(() => {
29	    window.location.hash = '#/settings';
30	  });
31	  await page.waitForURL('**/settings', { timeout: 15_000 });
32	
33	  // Read public key from the Settings page
34	  const pubKeyElement = page.locator('.settings-pubkey-value');
35	  await pubKeyElement.waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 10_000 });
36	  const publicKey = ((await pubKeyElement.textContent()) ?? '').trim();
37	  if (!publicKey || !publicKey.startsWith('0x')) {
38	    throw new Error(
39	      `Failed to extract public key from Settings page (got: "${publicKey.slice(0, 20)}")`
40	    );
41	  }
42	
43	  // Navigate back to files
44	  await page.evaluate(() => {
45	    window.location.hash = '#/files';
46	  });
47	  await page.waitForURL('**/files', { timeout: 15_000 });
48	
49	  return publicKey;
50	}
51	
52	/**
53	 * Create a test account with a random wallet identity and authenticated browser context.
54	 *
55	 * Uses @johanneskares/wallet-mock to inject an EIP-6963 mock provider,
56	 * then drives the real wallet -> SIWE -> Core Kit login flow through the UI.
57	 * Each account gets a unique random private key -> unique wallet address ->
58	 * unique backend userId -> fresh DKG identity on Sapphire Devnet.
59	 *
60	 * @param browser - Playwright browser instance
61	 * @param name - Human-readable name for this account (e.g., "alice")
62	 */
63	export async function createWalletTestAccount(
64	  browser: Browser,
65	  name: string
66	): Promise<WalletTestAccount> {
67	  const account = privateKeyToAccount(generatePrivateKey());
68	  const context = await browser.newContext();
69	
70	  try {
71	    const page = await context.newPage();
72	
73	    // Install mock wallet BEFORE navigating (EIP-6963 provider announcement)
74	    await setupMockWallet(page, account);
75	
76	    // Drive the real wallet login flow through the UI
77	    const result = await loginViaWallet(page, { timeout: 90_000 });
78	    if (result.outcome !== 'success') {
79	      throw new Error(
80	        `Wallet login for "${name}" did not reach /files (outcome: ${result.outcome})`
81	      );
82	    }
83	
84	    // loginViaWallet already waits for file list / empty state on success.
85	    // Extract public key from the Settings page UI (no Zustand access needed)
86	    const publicKey = await extractPublicKeyFromUI(page);
87	
88	    return {
89	      name,
90	      context,
91	      page,
92	      account,
93	      publicKey,
94	    };
95	  } catch (err) {
96	    await context.close().catch(() => undefined);
97	    throw err;
98	  }
99	}
100	
101	/**
102	 * Create multiple wallet-based test accounts sequentially.
103	 * Sequential creation avoids overwhelming the Web3Auth Sapphire Devnet.
104	 */
105	export async function createWalletTestAccounts(
106	  browser: Browser,
107	  names: string[]
108	): Promise<WalletTestAccount[]> {
109	  const accounts: WalletTestAccount[] = [];
110	  try {
111	    for (const name of names) {
112	      accounts.push(await createWalletTestAccount(browser, name));
113	    }
114	    return accounts;
115	  } catch (err) {
116	    // Clean up already-created accounts before re-throwing
117	    await closeWalletTestAccounts(accounts).catch(() => undefined);
118	    throw err;
119	  }
120	}
121	
122	/**
123	 * Delete accounts and close all wallet test account contexts.
124	 *
125	 * Calls deleteAccountViaPage for each account BEFORE closing the browser
126	 * context (the page must still be navigable to send API requests).
127	 * Each deletion is wrapped individually so one failure doesn't block others.
128	 */
129	export async function closeWalletTestAccounts(accounts: WalletTestAccount[]): Promise<void> {
130	  // Step 1: Delete accounts while pages are still open
131	  for (const account of accounts) {
132	    try {
133	      await deleteAccountViaPage(account.page);
134	    } catch (err) {
135	      console.warn(`[cleanup] Failed to delete account "${account.name}":`, err);
136	    }
137	  }
138	
139	  // Step 2: Close browser contexts (individually guarded so one failure doesn't block others)
140	  for (const account of accounts) {
141	    try {
142	      await account.context.close();
143	    } catch (err) {
144	      console.warn(`[cleanup] Failed to close context "${account.name}":`, err);
145	    }
146	  }
147	}
148	
149	/**
150	 * Navigate a wallet test account's page to the shared files view.
151	 * Navigates away first to force a component remount and fresh data fetch.
152	 */
153	export async function navigateToShared(account: WalletTestAccount): Promise<void> {
154	  const currentUrl = account.page.url();
155	  if (currentUrl.includes('/shared')) {
156	    await account.page.evaluate(() => {
157	      window.location.hash = '#/files';
158	    });
159	    await account.page.waitForURL('**/files', { timeout: 30000 });
160	  }
161	  await account.page.evaluate(() => {
162	    window.location.hash = '#/shared';
163	  });
164	  await account.page.waitForURL('**/shared', { timeout: 30000 });
165	}
166	
167	/**
168	 * Navigate a wallet test account's page to their own files.
169	 * Navigates away first to force a component remount and fresh data fetch.
170	 */
171	export async function navigateToFiles(account: WalletTestAccount): Promise<void> {
172	  const currentUrl = account.page.url();
173	  if (currentUrl.includes('/files')) {
174	    await account.page.evaluate(() => {
175	      window.location.hash = '#/shared';
176	    });
177	    await account.page.waitForURL('**/shared', { timeout: 30000 });
178	  }
179	  await account.page.evaluate(() => {
180	    window.location.hash = '#/files';
181	  });
182	  await account.page.waitForURL('**/files', { timeout: 30000 });
183	}
```

```
29	@Injectable()
30	export class IpnsService {
31	  private readonly logger = new Logger(IpnsService.name);
32	
33	  constructor(
34	    @InjectRepository(FolderIpns)
35	    private readonly folderIpnsRepository: Repository<FolderIpns>,
36	    private readonly delegatedRouting: DelegatedRoutingClient,
37	    @Inject(forwardRef(() => RepublishService))
38	    private readonly republishService: RepublishService,
39	    private readonly metricsService: MetricsService,
40	    private readonly sharesService: SharesService
41	  ) {}
42	
43	  /**
44	   * Publish a pre-signed IPNS record to the IPFS network via delegated routing
45	   * and track the folder in the database for TEE republishing
46	   */
47	  async publishRecord(
48	    userId: string,
49	    dto: PublishIpnsDto | PublishIpnsEntryDto,
50	    recordType: 'folder' | 'file' = 'folder'
51	  ): Promise<PublishIpnsResponseDto> {
52	    const endTimer = this.metricsService.ipfsIpnsDuration.startTimer({
53	      operation: 'publish',
54	      source: '',
55	    });
56	    let result = 'success';
57	
58	    try {
59	      // Validate base64 record
60	      let recordBytes: Uint8Array;
61	      try {
62	        recordBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(dto.record), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
63	      } catch {
64	        throw new BadRequestException('Invalid base64-encoded record');
65	      }
66	
67	      let publicKeyBytes: Uint8Array | undefined;
68	      if (dto.publicKey !== undefined) {
69	        try {
70	          publicKeyBytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(dto.publicKey), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
71	        } catch {
72	          throw new BadRequestException('Invalid base64-encoded publicKey');
73	        }
74	
75	        if (publicKeyBytes.length !== 32) {
76	          throw new BadRequestException('publicKey must be a raw 32-byte Ed25519 public key');
77	        }
78	
79	        // Verify the public key cryptographically derives to the claimed IPNS name
80	        const derivedName = await deriveIpnsName(publicKeyBytes);
81	        if (derivedName !== dto.ipnsName) {
82	          throw new BadRequestException('publicKey does not correspond to the given ipnsName');
83	        }
84	      }
85	
86	      // Save to DB first so resolve always has a fallback, even if delegated
87	      // routing fails (e.g. rate-limited, network error, DHT propagation delay).
88	      const folder = await this.upsertFolderIpns(
89	        userId,
90	        dto.ipnsName,
91	        dto.metadataCid,
92	        recordBytes,
93	        publicKeyBytes,
94	        dto.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
95	        dto.keyEpoch,
96	        recordType,
97	        dto.expectedSequenceNumber
98	      );
99	
100	      // Publish to delegated routing API (fire-and-forget — DB is the reliable source).
101	      // DHT propagation via someguy takes ~10-30s per record. Since the DB record
102	      // is already saved and resolveRecord() always checks/prefers DB data, there is
103	      // no need to block the API response on DHT propagation. Metrics are still
104	      // collected via the detached promise chain (catch + then).
105	      const publishStart = process.hrtime.bigint();
106	      this.delegatedRouting
107	        .publish(dto.ipnsName, recordBytes)
108	        .catch((error) => {
109	          this.logger.warn(
110	            `Delegated routing publish failed for ${dto.ipnsName}, DB record saved: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`
111	          );
112	          return 'error' as const;
113	        })
114	        .then((outcome) => {
115	          const publishElapsed = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - publishStart) / 1e9;
116	          this.metricsService.ipnsPublishDuration.observe(
117	            { outcome: outcome === 'error' ? 'error' : 'success' },
118	            publishElapsed
119	          );
120	        })
121	        .catch((error) => {
122	          this.logger.error(
123	            `Failed to record IPNS publish metrics for ${dto.ipnsName}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`
124	          );
125	        });
126	
127	      return {
128	        success: true,
129	        ipnsName: dto.ipnsName,
130	        sequenceNumber: folder.sequenceNumber,
131	      };
132	    } catch (error) {
133	      result = 'error';
134	      throw error;
135	    } finally {
136	      endTimer({ result });
137	    }
138	  }
139	
140	  /**
141	   * Batch publish multiple IPNS records with concurrency-limited processing.
142	   * Supports partial success: individual record failures do not fail the batch.
143	   * Processes up to 10 records concurrently.
144	   */
145	  async publishBatch(
146	    userId: string,
147	    dto: BatchPublishIpnsDto
148	  ): Promise<BatchPublishIpnsResponseDto> {
149	    const results: PublishIpnsResponseDto[] = [];
150	    let totalSucceeded = 0;
151	    let totalFailed = 0;
152	
153	    const CONCURRENCY = 10;
154	
155	    // Process records in batches of CONCURRENCY, delegating to publishRecord
156	    for (let i = 0; i < dto.records.length; i += CONCURRENCY) {
157	      const batch = dto.records.slice(i, i + CONCURRENCY);
158	
159	      const settled = await Promise.allSettled(
160	        batch.map((entry) => this.publishRecord(userId, entry, entry.recordType ?? 'folder'))
161	      );
162	
163	      for (let j = 0; j < settled.length; j++) {
164	        const result = settled[j];
165	        if (result.status === 'fulfilled') {
166	          results.push(result.value);
167	          totalSucceeded++;
168	        } else {
169	          const reason = result.reason;
170	          // If any record (especially a folder record) has a conflict, fail the entire batch
171	          if (reason instanceof ConflictException) {
172	            throw reason;
173	          }
174	          const ipnsName = batch[j]?.ipnsName ?? 'unknown';
175	          this.logger.warn(
176	            `Batch publish failed for ${ipnsName}: ${reason instanceof Error ? reason.message : String(reason)}`
177	          );
178	          results.push({
179	            success: false,
180	            ipnsName,
181	            sequenceNumber: '0',
182	          });
183	          totalFailed++;
184	        }
185	      }
186	    }
187	
188	    return { results, totalSucceeded, totalFailed };
189	  }
190	
191	  /**
192	   * Create or update a folder/file IPNS entry.
193	   * Handles both folder metadata and per-file metadata IPNS records.
194	   */
195	  private async upsertFolderIpns(
196	    userId: string,
197	    ipnsName: string,
198	    metadataCid: string,
199	    signedRecord: Uint8Array,
200	    publicKey?: Uint8Array,
201	    encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string,
202	    keyEpoch?: number,
203	    recordType: 'folder' | 'file' = 'folder',
204	    expectedSequenceNumber?: string
205	  ): Promise<FolderIpns> {
206	    let existing = await this.getFolderIpns(userId, ipnsName);
207	
208	    // If not the owner, check for active write share authorization.
209	    // When no owner record is found AND a write share exists, look up
210	    // the owner's FolderIpns by ipnsName. When no write share exists,
211	    // fall through to the create-new-entry path (owner's first publish).
212	    if (!existing) {
213	      const writeShare = await this.sharesService.findActiveWriteShare(userId, ipnsName);
214	      if (writeShare) {
215	        // Look up the sharer's FolderIpns record using their userId from the share
216	        existing = await this.folderIpnsRepository.findOne({
217	          where: { userId: writeShare.sharerId, ipnsName },
218	        });
219	        if (!existing) {
220	          throw new NotFoundException('IPNS name not found');
221	        }
222	      }
223	    }
224	
225	    // Conflict detection: when expectedSequenceNumber is provided, verify it matches
226	    if (existing && expectedSequenceNumber !== undefined) {
227	      const expected = BigInt(expectedSequenceNumber);
228	      const current = BigInt(existing.sequenceNumber);
229	      if (expected !== current) {
230	        throw new ConflictException({
231	          statusCode: 409,
232	          message: 'Sequence number mismatch: folder was modified by another device',
233	          currentSequenceNumber: existing.sequenceNumber,
234	          expectedSequenceNumber,
235	        });
236	      }
237	    }
238	
239	    if (existing) {
240	      if (publicKey && existing.publicKey && !existing.publicKey.equals(Buffer.from(publicKey))) {
241	        throw new BadRequestException('publicKey does not match the existing IPNS entry');
242	      }
243	
244	      // Update existing entry
245	      existing.latestCid = metadataCid;
246	      existing.sequenceNumber = (BigInt(existing.sequenceNumber) + 1n).toString();
247	      existing.signedRecord = Buffer.from(signedRecord);
248	      existing.publicKey = publicKey ? Buffer.from(publicKey) : existing.publicKey;
249	      existing.recordType = recordType;
250	      existing.updatedAt = new Date();
251	
252	      // Only update encrypted key if provided (e.g., on key rotation).
253	      // Guard: only the owner can update TEE enrollment fields — write-share
254	      // recipients must not overwrite encryptedIpnsPrivateKey or keyEpoch.
255	      if (encryptedIpnsPrivateKey && keyEpoch !== undefined && existing.userId === userId) {
256	        existing.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey = Buffer.from(encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, 'hex');
257	        existing.keyEpoch = keyEpoch;
258	      }
259	
260	      const saved = await this.folderIpnsRepository.save(existing);
261	
262	      // Auto-enroll for TEE republishing when encrypted key is provided.
263	      // Use existing.userId (the FolderIpns owner) for enrollment, not the
264	      // authenticated user — a write-share recipient publishes to the owner's record.
265	      if (encryptedIpnsPrivateKey && keyEpoch !== undefined && existing.userId === userId) {
266	        this.republishService
267	          .enrollFolder(
268	            existing.userId,
269	            ipnsName,
270	            Buffer.from(encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, 'hex'),
271	            keyEpoch,
272	            metadataCid,
273	            saved.sequenceNumber
274	          )
275	          .catch((err) =>
276	            this.logger.warn(
277	              `Failed to enroll ${recordType} ${ipnsName} for republishing: ${err.message}`
278	            )
279	          );
280	      }
281	
282	      return saved;
283	    }
284	
285	    // Create new entry — sequence starts at '1' to match the IPNS record
286	    // the client signed (clients compute newSeq = 0n + 1n = 1n for first publish).
287	    const folder = this.folderIpnsRepository.create({
288	      userId,
289	      ipnsName,
290	      latestCid: metadataCid,
291	      sequenceNumber: '1',
292	      signedRecord: Buffer.from(signedRecord),
293	      publicKey: publicKey ? Buffer.from(publicKey) : null,
294	      encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: encryptedIpnsPrivateKey
295	        ? Buffer.from(encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, 'hex')
296	        : null,
297	      keyEpoch: keyEpoch ?? null,
298	      isRoot: false, // Root folder is tracked in Vault entity
299	      recordType,
300	    });
301	
302	    const saved = await this.folderIpnsRepository.save(folder);
303	
304	    // Auto-enroll for TEE republishing when encrypted key is provided
305	    if (encryptedIpnsPrivateKey && keyEpoch !== undefined) {
306	      this.republishService
307	        .enrollFolder(
308	          userId,
309	          ipnsName,
310	          Buffer.from(encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, 'hex'),
311	          keyEpoch,
312	          metadataCid,
313	          saved.sequenceNumber
314	        )
315	        .catch((err) =>
316	          this.logger.warn(
317	            `Failed to enroll ${recordType} ${ipnsName} for republishing: ${err.message}`
318	          )
319	        );
320	    }
321	
322	    return saved;
323	  }
324	
325	  /**
326	   * Batch unenroll IPNS names from TEE republishing.
327	   * Called when files/folders are deleted to prevent orphaned enrollments.
328	   * Failures for individual names are logged but do not fail the batch.
329	   */
330	  async unenrollBatch(userId: string, ipnsNames: string[]): Promise<{ totalUnenrolled: number }> {
331	    const results = await Promise.allSettled(
332	      ipnsNames.map((ipnsName) => this.republishService.unenrollIpns(userId, ipnsName))
333	    );
334	    let unenrolled = 0;
335	    for (let i = 0; i < results.length; i++) {
336	      if (results[i].status === 'fulfilled') {
337	        unenrolled += (results[i] as PromiseFulfilledResult<number>).value;
338	      } else {
339	        const err = (results[i] as PromiseRejectedResult).reason;
340	        this.logger.warn(
341	          `Failed to unenroll ${ipnsNames[i]}: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : err}`
342	        );
343	      }
344	    }
345	    return { totalUnenrolled: unenrolled };
346	  }
347	
348	  /**
349	   * Get a folder IPNS entry by user and IPNS name
350	   */
351	  async getFolderIpns(userId: string, ipnsName: string): Promise<FolderIpns | null> {
352	    return this.folderIpnsRepository.findOne({
353	      where: { userId, ipnsName },
354	    });
355	  }
356	
357	  /**
358	   * Get all folder IPNS entries for a user (for TEE republishing)
359	   */
360	  async getAllFolderIpns(userId: string): Promise<FolderIpns[]> {
361	    return this.folderIpnsRepository.find({
362	      where: { userId },
363	      order: { createdAt: 'ASC' },
364	    });
365	  }
366	
367	  /**
368	   * Resolve an IPNS name to its current CID via delegated routing,
369	   * falling back to the DB-cached CID when delegated routing is unavailable
370	   * or when the record is not found in the DHT.
371	   * Returns null if the IPNS name is not found anywhere (404)
372	   */
373	  async resolveRecord(ipnsName: string): Promise<{
374	    cid: string;
375	    sequenceNumber: string;
376	    signatureV2?: string;
377	    data?: string;
378	    pubKey?: string;
379	  } | null> {
380	    const endTimer = this.metricsService.ipfsIpnsDuration.startTimer({
381	      operation: 'resolve',
382	    });
383	    let timerResult = 'success';
384	    let timerSource = 'network';
385	    const startTime = process.hrtime.bigint();
386	    let source = 'network';
387	    let resolveFound = false;
388	
389	    try {
390	      let result: {
391	        cid: string;
392	        sequenceNumber: string;
393	        signatureV2?: string;
394	        data?: string;
395	        pubKey?: string;
396	      } | null = null;
397	
398	      try {
399	        const recordBytes = await this.delegatedRouting.resolve(ipnsName);
400	        if (recordBytes) {
401	          result = this.parseIpnsRecordBytes(recordBytes);
402	          this.logger.debug(`IPNS name resolved successfully: ${ipnsName} -> ${result.cid}`);
403	        }
404	      } catch (error) {
405	        // Fall back to DB cache on BAD_GATEWAY (delegated routing failures)
406	        if (error instanceof HttpException && error.getStatus() === HttpStatus.BAD_GATEWAY) {
407	          this.logger.warn(`Delegated routing failed for ${ipnsName}, falling back to DB cache`);
408	          timerResult = 'error';
409	          source = 'db_cache';
410	        } else {
411	          timerResult = 'error';
412	          throw error;
413	        }
414	      }
415	
416	      // Always check DB cache — it's written synchronously during publish
417	      // and may be ahead of the network (delegated routing can serve stale records)
418	      const cached = await this.folderIpnsRepository.findOne({
419	        where: { ipnsName },
420	      });
421	      const cachedResult = this.parseCachedRecord(cached);
422	
423	      if (result && cached?.publicKey) {
424	        result = this.withCachedPublicKey(result, cached.publicKey);
425	      }
426	
427	      if (result && cachedResult) {
428	        // Both sources available — prefer the one with the higher sequence number
429	        const networkSeq = BigInt(result.sequenceNumber);
430	        const dbSeq = BigInt(cachedResult.sequenceNumber);
431	        if (dbSeq > networkSeq) {
432	          this.logger.log(
433	            `DB cache has newer sequence (${dbSeq} > ${networkSeq}) for ${ipnsName}, using DB: ${cachedResult.cid}`
434	          );
435	          timerSource = 'db';
436	          source = 'network_stale';
437	          resolveFound = true;
438	          return cachedResult;
439	        }
440	        // Equal sequence: enrich network result with cached signature fields if missing
441	        if (dbSeq === networkSeq && !result.signatureV2 && cachedResult.signatureV2) {
442	          result = {
443	            ...result,
444	            signatureV2: cachedResult.signatureV2,
445	            data: cachedResult.data,
446	            pubKey: result.pubKey ?? cachedResult.pubKey,
447	          };
448	        }
449	        timerSource = 'network';
450	        resolveFound = true;
451	        return result;
452	      }
453	
454	      if (result) {
455	        timerSource = 'network';
456	        resolveFound = true;
457	        return result;
458	      }
459	
460	      // Delegated routing returned null (404) or threw BAD_GATEWAY — try DB cache
461	      if (cachedResult) {
462	        this.logger.log(`Resolved ${ipnsName} from DB cache: ${cachedResult.cid}`);
463	        timerSource = 'db';
464	        source = 'db_cache';
465	        resolveFound = true;
466	        return cachedResult;
467	      }
468	
469	      return null;
470	    } catch (error) {
471	      timerResult = 'error';
472	      throw error;
473	    } finally {
474	      endTimer({ result: timerResult, source: timerSource });
475	      if (resolveFound) {
476	        const elapsed = Number(process.hrtime.bigint() - startTime) / 1e9;
477	        this.metricsService.ipnsResolveDuration.observe({ source, outcome: timerResult }, elapsed);
478	      }
479	    }
480	  }
481	
482	  /**
483	   * Parse an IPNS record to extract CID and sequence number
484	   * Uses inline protobuf decoder — no external dependencies
485	   */
486	  private parseIpnsRecordBytes(recordBytes: Uint8Array): {
487	    cid: string;
488	    sequenceNumber: string;
489	    signatureV2?: string;
490	    data?: string;
491	    pubKey?: string;
492	  } {
493	    try {
494	      const record = parseIpnsRecord(recordBytes);
495	
496	      // Extract CID from the Value field (format: /ipfs/<cid>)
497	      const valuePath = record.value;
498	      const cidMatch = valuePath.match(/\/ipfs\/([a-zA-Z0-9]+)/);
499	      if (!cidMatch) {
500	        this.logger.error('Failed to extract CID from IPNS record value');
501	        throw new HttpException('Invalid IPNS record format', HttpStatus.BAD_GATEWAY);
502	      }
503	
504	      const cid = cidMatch[1];
505	      const sequenceNumber = String(record.sequence ?? 0n);
506	
507	      // Base64-encode signature fields if present
508	      const signatureV2 = record.signatureV2
509	        ? Buffer.from(record.signatureV2).toString('base64')
510	        : undefined;
511	      const data = record.data ? Buffer.from(record.data).toString('base64') : undefined;
512	      const pubKey = record.pubKey ? Buffer.from(record.pubKey).toString('base64') : undefined;
513	
514	      this.logger.debug(`Parsed IPNS record: cid=${cid}, sequenceNumber=${sequenceNumber}`);
515	      return { cid, sequenceNumber, signatureV2, data, pubKey };
516	    } catch (error) {
517	      if (error instanceof HttpException) {
518	        throw error;
519	      }
520	      this.logger.error(`Failed to parse IPNS record: ${error}`);
521	      throw new HttpException('Invalid IPNS record format', HttpStatus.BAD_GATEWAY);
522	    }
523	  }
524	
525	  private parseCachedRecord(cached: FolderIpns | null): {
526	    cid: string;
527	    sequenceNumber: string;
528	    signatureV2?: string;
529	    data?: string;
530	    pubKey?: string;
531	  } | null {
532	    if (!cached?.latestCid) {
533	      return null;
534	    }
535	
536	    if (cached.signedRecord) {
537	      try {
538	        const parsed = this.withCachedPublicKey(
539	          this.parseIpnsRecordBytes(cached.signedRecord),
540	          cached.publicKey ?? undefined
541	        );
542	        // Use the DB column's sequenceNumber as authoritative — it is always
543	        // incremented by upsertFolderIpns, while the record bytes may contain
544	        // the client's pre-increment value (e.g. sequence 0 on first publish).
545	        return { ...parsed, sequenceNumber: cached.sequenceNumber };
546	      } catch (error) {
547	        const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
548	        this.logger.warn(`Failed to parse cached signed record for ${cached.ipnsName}: ${message}`);
549	      }
550	    }
551	
552	    return { cid: cached.latestCid, sequenceNumber: cached.sequenceNumber };
553	  }
554	
555	  private withCachedPublicKey(
556	    result: {
557	      cid: string;
558	      sequenceNumber: string;
559	      signatureV2?: string;
560	      data?: string;
561	      pubKey?: string;
562	    },
563	    publicKey?: Buffer
564	  ): {
565	    cid: string;
566	    sequenceNumber: string;
567	    signatureV2?: string;
568	    data?: string;
569	    pubKey?: string;
570	  } {
571	    if (result.pubKey || !result.signatureV2 || !result.data || !publicKey) {
572	      return result;
573	    }
574	
575	    return {
576	      ...result,
577	      pubKey: publicKey.toString('base64'),
578	    };
579	  }
580	}
581
```

```
1	/**
2	 * Lightweight inline IPNS record parser.
3	 *
4	 * The IPNS record is a protobuf message. We extract these fields:
5	 *   field 1 (Value)       – length-delimited bytes, decoded as UTF-8 string
6	 *   field 5 (Sequence)    – varint, decoded as bigint
7	 *   field 7 (PubKey)      – length-delimited, protobuf-wrapped Ed25519 public key
8	 *   field 8 (SignatureV2) – length-delimited, Ed25519 signature bytes
9	 *   field 9 (Data)        – length-delimited, CBOR-encoded signed data
10	 *
11	 * SignatureV2 (field 8) and data (field 9) are returned independently.
12	 * The public key (field 7) may be omitted by some publishers, in which case
13	 * callers can supplement it from trusted cached metadata.
14	 *
15	 * Wire format reference: https://protobuf.dev/programming-guides/encoding/
16	 */
17	export interface ParsedIpnsRecord {
18	  value: string;
19	  sequence: bigint;
20	  /** Ed25519 signature bytes (protobuf field 8) */
21	  signatureV2?: Uint8Array;
22	  /** CBOR-encoded record data that was signed (protobuf field 9) */
23	  data?: Uint8Array;
24	  /** Raw 32-byte Ed25519 public key extracted from protobuf-wrapped libp2p key (field 7) */
25	  pubKey?: Uint8Array;
26	}
27	
28	function readVarint(buf: Uint8Array, offset: number): [bigint, number] {
29	  let result = 0n;
30	  let shift = 0n;
31	  let pos = offset;
32	  while (pos < buf.length) {
33	    const byte = buf[pos];
34	    result |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;
35	    pos++;
36	    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) return [result, pos];
37	    shift += 7n;
38	    if (shift > 63n) throw new Error('Varint too long');
39	  }
40	  throw new Error('Unexpected end of buffer reading varint');
41	}
42	
43	/**
44	 * Extract raw 32-byte Ed25519 public key from a protobuf-wrapped libp2p public key.
45	 *
46	 * The libp2p public key protobuf wrapping is:
47	 *   [0x08, 0x01, 0x12, 0x20, ...32 bytes of Ed25519 pubkey]
48	 *
49	 * - 0x08 0x01 = field 1 (KeyType), varint, value 1 (Ed25519)
50	 * - 0x12 0x20 = field 2 (Data), length-delimited, 32 bytes
51	 *
52	 * @returns Raw 32-byte Ed25519 public key, or undefined if format is unexpected
53	 */
54	function extractEd25519PubKey(wrappedKey: Uint8Array): Uint8Array | undefined {
55	  // Standard libp2p Ed25519 public key is 36 bytes: 4-byte protobuf prefix + 32-byte key
56	  if (
57	    wrappedKey.length === 36 &&
58	    wrappedKey[0] === 0x08 &&
59	    wrappedKey[1] === 0x01 &&
60	    wrappedKey[2] === 0x12 &&
61	    wrappedKey[3] === 0x20
62	  ) {
63	    return wrappedKey.subarray(4);
64	  }
65	  return undefined;
66	}
67	
68	export function parseIpnsRecord(buf: Uint8Array): ParsedIpnsRecord {
69	  let value: string | undefined;
70	  let sequence = 0n;
71	  let signatureV2: Uint8Array | undefined;
72	  let data: Uint8Array | undefined;
73	  let rawPubKey: Uint8Array | undefined;
74	
75	  let pos = 0;
76	  while (pos < buf.length) {
77	    const [tag, nextPos] = readVarint(buf, pos);
78	    pos = nextPos;
79	    const fieldNumber = Number(tag >> 3n);
80	    const wireType = Number(tag & 0x7n);
81	
82	    if (wireType === 0) {
83	      // Varint
84	      const [val, np] = readVarint(buf, pos);
85	      pos = np;
86	      if (fieldNumber === 5) sequence = val;
87	    } else if (wireType === 2) {
88	      // Length-delimited
89	      const [len, np] = readVarint(buf, pos);
90	      pos = np;
91	      const end = pos + Number(len);
92	      if (end > buf.length) throw new Error('Length-delimited field exceeds buffer');
93	      if (fieldNumber === 1) {
94	        value = new TextDecoder().decode(buf.subarray(pos, end));
95	      } else if (fieldNumber === 7) {
96	        // pubKey - protobuf-wrapped libp2p public key; extract raw Ed25519 key
97	        const wrappedKey = buf.slice(pos, end);
98	        rawPubKey = extractEd25519PubKey(wrappedKey);
99	      } else if (fieldNumber === 8) {
100	        // signatureV2 - Ed25519 signature bytes
101	        signatureV2 = buf.slice(pos, end);
102	      } else if (fieldNumber === 9) {
103	        // data - CBOR-encoded record data that was signed
104	        data = buf.slice(pos, end);
105	      }
106	      pos = end;
107	    } else if (wireType === 5) {
108	      if (pos + 4 > buf.length) throw new Error('Buffer underflow reading 32-bit field');
109	      pos += 4;
110	    } else if (wireType === 1) {
111	      if (pos + 8 > buf.length) throw new Error('Buffer underflow reading 64-bit field');
112	      pos += 8;
113	    } else {
114	      throw new Error(`Unsupported wire type ${wireType}`);
115	    }
116	  }
117	
118	  if (value === undefined) {
119	    throw new Error('IPNS record missing Value field');
120	  }
121	
122	  return {
123	    value,
124	    sequence,
125	    signatureV2,
126	    data,
127	    pubKey: rawPubKey,
128	  };
129	}
130
```

```
38	/** Parse an invite URL to extract token and ephemeral key */
39	function parseInviteUrl(url: string): { token: string; ephemeralKey: string } {
40	  // URL format: http://localhost:5173/#/invite/TOKEN?key=KEY
41	  const hashPart = url.split('#')[1]; // /invite/TOKEN?key=KEY
42	  const [path, query] = hashPart.split('?');
43	  const token = path.split('/invite/')[1];
44	  const params = new URLSearchParams(query);
45	  const ephemeralKey = params.get('key')!;
46	  return { token, ephemeralKey };
47	}
```

```
1	/**
2	 * Mock Delegated Routing Service for E2E Testing
3	 *
4	 * Implements the IPFS delegated routing API for IPNS records:
5	 * - PUT /routing/v1/ipns/{name} - Store an IPNS record
6	 * - GET /routing/v1/ipns/{name} - Retrieve an IPNS record
7	 *
8	 * Records are stored in-memory and reset when the service restarts.
9	 * This allows E2E tests to run without polluting the public IPFS DHT
10	 * and avoids sequence number conflicts from repeated test runs.
11	 */
12	
13	import Fastify from 'fastify';
14	
15	const fastify = Fastify({
16	  logger: {
17	    level: process.env.LOG_LEVEL ?? 'info',
18	    transport:
19	      process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production'
20	        ? { target: 'pino-pretty', options: { colorize: true } }
21	        : undefined,
22	  },
23	});
24	
25	// CORS headers for browser-based recovery tool testing (read-only access)
26	fastify.addHook('onRequest', async (request, reply) => {
27	  reply.header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*');
28	  reply.header('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET, OPTIONS');
29	  reply.header('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Content-Type, Accept');
30	  if (request.method === 'OPTIONS') {
31	    return reply.status(204).send();
32	  }
33	});
34	
35	// In-memory storage for IPNS records
36	// Key: IPNS name (k51... or bafzaa...), Value: raw record bytes
37	const ipnsRecords = new Map<string, Buffer>();
38	
39	// Health check endpoint
40	fastify.get('/health', async () => {
41	  return { status: 'ok', records: ipnsRecords.size };
42	});
43	
44	// GET /routing/v1/ipns/{name} - Retrieve IPNS record
45	fastify.get<{ Params: { name: string } }>('/routing/v1/ipns/:name', async (request, reply) => {
46	  const { name } = request.params;
47	
48	  const record = ipnsRecords.get(name);
49	  if (!record) {
50	    return reply.status(404).send({
51	      error: 'record not found',
52	      name,
53	    });
54	  }
55	
56	  fastify.log.info({ name, size: record.length }, 'Retrieved IPNS record');
57	
58	  return reply.status(200).header('Content-Type', 'application/vnd.ipfs.ipns-record').send(record);
59	});
60	
61	// PUT /routing/v1/ipns/{name} - Store IPNS record
62	fastify.put<{ Params: { name: string } }>('/routing/v1/ipns/:name', async (request, reply) => {
63	  const { name } = request.params;
64	
65	  // Get raw body as Buffer
66	  const body = request.body as Buffer;
67	  if (!body || body.length === 0) {
68	    return reply.status(400).send({
69	      error: 'empty request body',
70	    });
71	  }
72	
73	  // Store the record (overwrites any existing record)
74	  // Note: We intentionally don't validate sequence numbers here.
75	  // This allows tests to reset state by simply restarting the service.
76	  ipnsRecords.set(name, body);
77	
78	  fastify.log.info(
79	    { name, size: body.length, totalRecords: ipnsRecords.size },
80	    'Stored IPNS record'
81	  );
82	
83	  return reply.status(200).send({ ok: true });
84	});
85	
86	// Configure to accept raw binary bodies
87	fastify.addContentTypeParser(
88	  'application/vnd.ipfs.ipns-record',
89	  { parseAs: 'buffer' },
90	  async (_request: unknown, payload: Buffer) => payload
91	);
92	
93	// Also accept application/octet-stream for compatibility
94	fastify.addContentTypeParser(
95	  'application/octet-stream',
96	  { parseAs: 'buffer' },
97	  async (_request: unknown, payload: Buffer) => payload
98	);
99	
100	// Reset endpoint for tests - clears all stored records
101	fastify.post('/reset', async () => {
102	  const count = ipnsRecords.size;
103	  ipnsRecords.clear();
104	  fastify.log.info({ clearedRecords: count }, 'Reset all IPNS records');
105	  return { ok: true, clearedRecords: count };
106	});
107	
108	// Start server
109	const start = async () => {
110	  const host = process.env.HOST ?? '0.0.0.0';
111	  const port = parseInt(process.env.PORT ?? '3001', 10);
112	
113	  try {
114	    await fastify.listen({ host, port });
115	    fastify.log.info(`Mock IPNS routing service listening on http://${host}:${port}`);
116	    fastify.log.info('Endpoints:');
117	    fastify.log.info('  GET  /health - Health check');
118	    fastify.log.info('  GET  /routing/v1/ipns/:name - Get IPNS record');
119	    fastify.log.info('  PUT  /routing/v1/ipns/:name - Store IPNS record');
120	    fastify.log.info('  POST /reset - Clear all records');
121	  } catch (err) {
122	    fastify.log.error(err);
123	    process.exit(1);
124	  }
125	};
126	
127	start();
128
```

```
Perfect! I've found and read the file. Here's my report on how the `resolve` method works:

## Delegated Routing Client - `resolve` Method

**File Location:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/api/src/ipns/delegated-routing.client.ts`

### How the `resolve` Method Works

**Main Entry Point (lines 75-93):**
The public `resolve` method takes an IPNS name as a string and:
1. URL-encodes the IPNS name using `encodeURIComponent()`
2. Constructs the path: `/routing/v1/ipns/{encodedName}`
3. Attempts resolution via the primary backend first
4. Falls back to a secondary backend if configured and the primary fails
5. Returns the raw record bytes or `null` if not found

**Fetching IPNS Records (lines 191-302):**
The private `executeResolve` method handles the actual HTTP request with retry logic:
- Makes a GET request to the routing service with the `Accept: application/vnd.ipfs.ipns-record` header
- Implements exponential backoff with up to 3 retry attempts (configurable `maxRetries`)
- Uses a 5-second timeout on each request (`requestTimeoutMs = 5_000`)

**Response Handling:**
- **Success (200):** Returns `Uint8Array` containing the raw response bytes via `new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer())` (line 212)
- **Not Found (404):** Returns `null` (line 228) - treated as a non-error condition
- **Rate Limiting (429):** Retries with delay based on `Retry-After` header or exponential backoff
- **Other Errors:** Throws an `HttpException` with status `BAD_GATEWAY` after all retries are exhausted

### Return Format
The `resolve` method returns either:
- **`Uint8Array`** - The raw IPNS record bytes fetched from the routing service
- **`null`** - When the IPNS name is not found (404 response)

The bytes are extracted directly from the HTTP response body as an `ArrayBuffer` and converted to `Uint8Array`.
```
